tokenizer 训练算法中两个长期混在一起的因素：优化目标和搜索过程。BPE 通常被理解为压缩目标更好，UnigramLM 被理解为似然目标较弱；但二者同时也分别使用 bottom-up merge 和 top-down pruning。过去只比较 BPE 与 UnigramLM，无法判断性能差异来自目标，还是来自搜索启发式。","importance":"这是对 LLM 预训练基础组件的一次干净拆解。tokenizer 会改变序列长度、token 频率分布、embedding/softmax 参数利用率、跨语言覆盖和训练 loss 的可比性。论文的主要结论是：在 bits-per-byte 这类语言建模指标上，搜索过程的影响往往强于目标本身，bottom-up tokenizer 在多数设置下更好；但在小词表场景，似然目标仍有优势。这个结果会影响实际 tokenizer 设计：不应简单把 BPE 的优势归因于压缩目标，也不应把 UnigramLM 的问题归因于 unigram likelihood。更合理的研究方向是分别设计目标函数与搜索过程，而不是把现有算法当作不可分解的整体。","technical_approach":"论文构造了一个 2×2 实验设计，横轴是目标函数，纵轴是搜索过程。已有两个角是 BPE 和 UnigramLM：BPE 使用压缩目标和 bottom-up 合并；UnigramLM 使用 unigram log-likelihood 目标和 top-down 剪枝。作者补上另外两个角：BottomUpLL 用类似 BPE 的自底向上搜索，但每次选择以语料 unigram log-likelihood 改善为准；TopDownComp 用类似 UnigramLM 的自顶向下剪枝，但剪枝准则改成尽量保持压缩收益。这样可以把“优化什么”和“怎么搜索”分离。这个设计比直接提出一个新 tokenizer 更有价值，因为它把 BPE 与 UnigramLM 的差异拆成可实验验证的因子。需要注意的是，tokenizer 学习问题通常是 NP-hard，实际算法依赖启发式搜索，因此搜索路径本身会决定最终词表质量；这也是论文结论成立的关键。","experiments":"实验训练了使用四类 tokenizer 的语言模型，并改变模型大小、词表大小和领域设置，包括 English-only 与 multilingual。主要外部指标是 bits-per-byte，另有 BLiMP 语法判断等评测。最清晰的结果是排序关系：在 BPB 上，bottom-up 方法几乎在所有条件下占优；BPE 通常优于 TopDownComp，BottomUpLL 通常优于 UnigramLM。这说明搜索过程比压缩/似然目标更能解释 BPB 差异。这个排序不稳定地迁移到 BLiMP：语法判断上没有哪个目标或搜索过程形成一致优势。小词表设置下，目标函数影响变得更明显，likelihood-based tokenizer 往往优于 compression-based tokenizer。这个现象合理：词表容量不足时，压缩目标倾向于短期减少 token 数，而似然目标更直接考虑分词概率分布，对稀缺词表预算的分配更敏感。给出的材料没有包含可核验的具体参数量、训练 token 数或每个表格的数值，因此不应复述未见数字。","reading_guide":"建议精读，预计 60–90 分钟。第一优先级是 Fig. 1 和方法章节，理解 2×2 设计如何把目标函数与搜索过程解耦。第二优先级是 tokenizer objective 的形式化定义：compression 与 unigram likelihood 分别在优化什么，它们与最终 LM loss 的关系并不相同。第三优先级是实验表格中 BPB 随词表大小、模型大小、语言设置变化的趋势，尤其要看 BPE vs TopDownComp、BottomUpLL vs UnigramLM 这两组成对比较，因为它们固定目标或固定搜索过程。BLiMP 结果也值得看，它提醒 tokenizer 对 next-token loss 的好处不一定等价于语言现象判断的好处。值得深挖的问题有三个：bottom-up 搜索为什么更适配神经 LM；小词表下 likelihood objective 的优势能否扩展到 byte-level 或 multilingual tokenizer；这些结论在更大规模模型和更长训练 token 预算下是否保持。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks which part of a tokenizer learning algorithm actually matters: the objective being optimized or the search procedure used to approximate it. BPE and UnigramLM differ on both axes at once. BPE uses a compression objective with bottom-up merging, while UnigramLM uses a unigram likelihood objective with top-down pruning. A direct BPE-versus-UnigramLM comparison therefore cannot attribute downstream LM differences to either factor cleanly.","importance":"This is a useful foundation-model paper because tokenization is not a preprocessing detail. It changes sequence length, token frequency distributions, embedding and softmax allocation, multilingual coverage, and the meaning of LM loss comparisons. The central finding is that, for bits-per-byte, the search procedure often explains more than the objective: bottom-up tokenizers perform best in most settings. At small vocabulary sizes, however, likelihood-based objectives still matter. The practical implication is that BPE’s empirical strength should not be credited only to compression, and UnigramLM’s weaknesses should not be blamed only on the likelihood objective. Objective design and search design should be studied separately.","technical_approach":"The paper completes a 2-by-2 design space. The two existing algorithms occupy two corners: BPE is compression plus bottom-up merging, and UnigramLM is log-likelihood plus top-down pruning. The authors add BottomUpLL, which keeps the BPE-like bottom-up construction but selects changes according to unigram log-likelihood, and TopDownComp, which keeps the UnigramLM-like top-down pruning procedure but optimizes a compression criterion. This isolates what is being optimized from how the vocabulary is searched. The distinction matters because exact tokenizer learning is generally hard, so the heuristic path can have a large effect on the final vocabulary even when the nominal objective is fixed.","experiments":"The experiments train language models with tokenizers from all four algorithms while varying model size, vocabulary size, and domain, including English-only and multilingual settings. The main extrinsic metric is bits-per-byte, with additional evaluation on grammaticality judgments such as BLiMP. The strongest result is a consistent ordering for BPB: bottom-up methods usually win. BPE tends to outperform TopDownComp, and BottomUpLL tends to outperform UnigramLM. This means the search procedure is a stronger driver of BPB than the compression-versus-likelihood objective in most tested conditions. The same ordering does not consistently hold for BLiMP, where neither axis cleanly dominates. At small vocabulary sizes, the objective becomes more important, with likelihood-based tokenizers outperforming compression-based ones. The supplied text does not provide enough reliable tables to report exact parameter counts, token budgets, or numerical deltas.","reading_guide":"Read carefully, about 60–90 minutes. Start with the 2-by-2 framing and the definitions of the four tokenizer algorithms. Then inspect the formal objectives for compression and unigram likelihood, since they optimize different proxies and neither is the LM training objective itself. The most informative experimental comparisons are BPE versus TopDownComp and BottomUpLL versus UnigramLM, because these pairs hold one axis fixed while changing the other. The BPB trends across vocabulary size, model size, and English versus multilingual data are the main evidence. The BLiMP results are also useful because they show that lower byte-level LM loss does not automatically imply better grammaticality judgments. Follow-up questions worth investigating are why bottom-up search better matches neural LM training, whether the small-vocabulary advantage of likelihood objectives extends to byte-level and multilingual tokenizers, and whether the conclusions hold at much larger scale.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看实验设计；它把 tokenizer 里常被混在一起的目标函数和搜索过程拆开，可能改变 BPE/UnigramLM 取舍的默认解释。","en":"Read the experimental design; it separates objective from search in tokenization and may change the usual explanation for BPE versus UnigramLM behavior."},"metadata":{"institutions":["ETH Zurich","EPFL","Affiliation:EPFL","Affiliation:ETH Zürich"],"authors":["Ahmetcan Yavuz","Clara Meister","Tiago Pimentel"],"notable_authors":["Clara Meister","Tiago Pimentel"],"tags":["tokenization","pretraining-data","BPE","UnigramLM","bits-per-byte"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19145","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.19145","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.18878","title":"Preventing Model Collapse: A Fisher-Rao Perspective on the Dynamics of Training with Synthetic Data","abstract":"Large Language Models (LLMs) are now routinely trained using synthetic data, since high-quality human data has been exhausted by the ever increasing needs of larger and larger models. 的任务类型，给每类任务标注当前自动化水平，再聚合成总体指标。自动化等级采用 Epoch AI 的 Automation Level 标尺：AL0 表示没有 AI 参与，AL3 表示 AI 在人类密切指导下完成大块工作，AL4 表示 AI 可以从高层指令出发主导完成大部分端到端任务，AL5 表示无人工介入的完全自治。第二是 agent oversight 指标。文章关心的不只是 agent 是否能完成任务，而是 agent 的行动是否被覆盖、审查和拦截。指标包括 coverage，即有多少 agent action 经过在线或离线监控；review latency，即行动发生到自动监控和人工复核之间的延迟；escalation rate，即被阻断、重定向或标记复核的活动比例。第三是 compute allocation。文章把算力看作最容易被外部验证的研发输入之一，用于刻画算力在模型训练、客户服务、安全工作之间的分配。这个方向与 capability eval 互补：capability eval 测输出，compute 和研发自动化指标测输入与生产过程。","experiments":"文章给出的是内部测量快照，不是常规实验。关键数字有三组。第一，Claude 在被测 AI R&D 工作中没有任何子集达到完全自治；也就是没有达到 AL5。第二，Claude 在 26% 的 Anthropic AI R&D 工作中达到“AI leads”，即 AL4；达到或超过“AI collaborates”的工作比例超过 90%，即 AL3+。第三，截至 2026 年 8 月，Anthropic 最常用的内部平台上约有 30,000 个 agent 同时从事研究和工程工作。文章明确说这些 agent 监督数据只覆盖该平台，不代表所有内部系统。它还指出现有测量有两个限制：缺少跨 lab 统一方法；用自家模型评估自家系统可能带来相关错误，需要第三方或其他开发者模型进行验证。","reading_guide":"精读重点应放在三处。第一，看 AI-led R&D 部分，尤其是 AL0 到 AL5 的定义、任务枚举和聚合逻辑。这决定了 26% AL4 和 90% AL3+ 是否有可比性。第二，看 agent oversight 部分，重点理解 coverage、review latency、escalation rate 三个指标如何对应 agent 规模化后的风险。这里的关键不是单个 agent 的失败率，而是大规模并发下低概率事件会变成常态。第三，看 compute allocation 的动机，因为算力分配可能成为连接研发输入、模型能力和外部审计的稳定锚点。值得深挖的问题是：R&D Automation Index 是否能和实际训练吞吐、实验成功率、模型能力增量建立量化关系；以及固定 testing window 是否会成为 AI-assisted pretraining 的必要制度约束。","authors_note":"Anthropic 发布的机构博客，未列出个人作者。文中使用 Epoch AI 的 Automation Level 标尺，并提到 METR 对内部监控系统的独立 red-team 工作。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This Anthropic post addresses a measurement problem: how external stakeholders can track the pace of frontier AI development inside labs, especially the degree to which AI systems are helping build their successors. It proposes process-level metrics for AI-led R&D, oversight of internal agents, and compute allocation.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model research is not a new training method, but a lab-level measurement framework. Pretraining progress is usually discussed in terms of compute, data, architectures, optimization, and benchmarks. If AI systems increasingly design experiments, write training code, analyze runs, and operate evaluation workflows, then the effective rate of model development changes. That affects iteration speed, scaling assumptions, safety review windows, and the interpretation of compute-to-capability curves.","technical_approach":"The post proposes three measurement tracks. The first is the Anthropic R&D Automation Index. Anthropic catalogs categories of AI R&D work, assigns an automation level to each category, and aggregates the ratings. The scale follows Epoch AI’s Automation Level framework: AL0 means no AI involvement; AL3 means AI collaborates under close human direction; AL4 means AI leads most of an end-to-end task from a high-level prompt; AL5 means fully autonomous execution without a human in the loop. The second track measures oversight of internal agents. It uses coverage, review latency, and escalation rate to describe whether agent actions are monitored, how quickly they are reviewed, and how often they are blocked or flagged. The third track is compute allocation. Compute is treated as one of the more verifiable inputs to AI development, and allocation across model-building, serving, and safety work is proposed as a useful signal of a lab’s operational focus.","experiments":"The post reports internal measurement snapshots rather than controlled experiments. Claude is not fully autonomous for any measured subset of Anthropic AI R&D work. Claude reaches the “AI leads” level for 26% of Anthropic’s AI R&D work, and the share at or above “AI collaborates” is above 90%. As of August 2026, Anthropic’s most-used internal platform had roughly 30,000 agents doing research and engineering work at any given time. The agent measurements cover only that platform. The post also states two limitations: cross-lab comparison lacks a shared methodology, and using a lab’s own models to evaluate its systems can introduce correlated errors. Third-party verification or carefully constrained cross-lab evaluation is proposed as a remedy.","reading_guide":"Read the AI-led R&D section closely, especially the AL0–AL5 definitions and how task categories are aggregated. That is where the 26% AL4 and 90% AL3+ numbers get their meaning. Then read the agent oversight section for the coverage, latency, and escalation metrics; these are more operationally useful than a simple count of agents. The compute allocation discussion is useful for thinking about auditable inputs to future scaling analyses. The deepest follow-up question is whether an R&D automation index can be linked quantitatively to experiment throughput, training iteration speed, and capability gains.","authors_note":"Institutional post by Anthropic; no individual authors are listed. Biology is a domain where per-request refusal is often too blunt: the same surface form can describe vaccine research or harmful pathogen work. Anthropic’s design shifts part of the safety boundary from real-time request blocking to identity verification, declared use cases, and offline behavioral monitoring. High-risk grants are available for Claude Opus 5 and Claude Sonnet 5; high-risk access to Claude Mythos remains limited to a small set of additionally vetted entities while Anthropic works with the US government on broader access. sco","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T08:31:57Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2609_18310.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇技术报告解决的是区域语言能力如何可靠注入强基座模型的问题，重点是东南亚语言。论文没有从头训练一个新模型，而是在 NVIDIA Nemotron 3 的 30B-A3B 和 120B-A12B MoE 基座上做继续预训练和后训练，目标是在保留原有推理、代码、长上下文和多语言能力的同时，补足东南亚语言、文化和任务覆盖上的短板。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要在于区域化继续预训练 recipe，而不是新架构。它给出一个清晰案例：对低资源或中资源语言群，单纯依赖大规模通用多语言预训练并不够，150B 高质量定向 token 加上后训练可以带来稳定收益；并且这个收益在 30B-A3B 到 120B-A12B 两个稀疏激活规模上都成立。更重要的是，论文把 continued pretraining 和 post-training 分成两个可观察阶段，并发布 base 与 instruction/post-trained checkpoint，这有利于研究者区分“语言知识注入”与“交互行为对齐”的贡献。","technical_approach":"方法由两段组成。第一段是继续预训练：以 Nemotron 3 为初始化，保留原 tokenizer，不改模型上下文窗口和主体结构，在约 150B 高质量 token 上训练。数据覆盖问答、推理、代码、多语言翻译，并强调英语与东南亚语言。保留 tokenizer 是一个稳健选择，避免词表变更带来的 embedding 迁移和兼容性问题，但代价是对部分东南亚文字系统可能仍有分词效率损失。第二段是后训练：在继续预训练后的 base checkpoint 上，使用统一训练管线，将 SFT 与 online on-policy distillation 结合。OPD 的关键区别是模型不是只拟合静态指令数据，而是在当前策略下生成交互轨迹，再从这些轨迹获得监督信号。这更接近模型实际部署时会遇到的分布，也能减少纯离线蒸馏中 teacher/student 行为分布不匹配的问题。架构上，30B-A3B 使用 Mamba2-Transformer hybrid MoE，约 30B 总参数、3B 激活参数；120B-A12B 是更大稀疏激活模型，约 120B 总参数、12B 激活参数。30B 训练使用 Megatron Bridge，120B 使用 NVIDIA NeMo AutoModel。报告的创新点更偏工程系统和训练配方：在不重训基座、不改 tokenizer 的条件下，通过定向数据和在线后训练实现区域能力迁移。","experiments":"核心评测是更新后的 SEA-HELM，覆盖七种东南亚语言，并扩展了数据覆盖和评测方法。30B-A3B post-trained 模型相对对应 Nemotron 3 基线，SEA-HELM overall SEA score 从 46.06 提升到 51.57，绝对提升 5.51 分。能力维度上，指令遵循、自然语言生成、推理和语言理解均有提升。120B-A12B 的提升更明显，overall SEA score 从 49.30 提升到 63.44，绝对提升 14.14 分。报告特别指出 Tamil 和 Burmese 上的收益较大，并且在文化、指令遵循、知识、推理和语言理解维度上更一致。这个结果说明区域继续预训练在更大稀疏模型上没有失效，反而可能因为基座容量更大而更容易吸收定向语言与文化数据。需要注意的是，提供内容中没有看到完整的数据配比、token 质量过滤标准、训练步数、学习率曲线、各阶段消融和灾难性遗忘评估，因此很难精确判断 150B token 中哪类数据贡献最大。","reading_guide":"建议精读模型架构和训练流程部分，重点看四个 checkpoint 的关系：30B-A3B-Base、30B-A3B、120B-A12B-Base、120B-A12B。这个设计让 continued pretraining 与 post-training 的效果可以拆开分析。其次看数据描述，尤其是 150B token 的来源、质量控制、语言比例、QA/推理/代码/翻译数据的混合方式；如果正文或附录给出更细配比，这是最值得深挖的部分。第三看 SEA-HELM 的更新说明和分语言、分能力结果，尤其是 Tamil、Burmese 等提升较大的语言，判断收益来自语言建模能力、文化知识，还是指令模板适配。第四看 OPD 管线，关注在线轨迹如何生成、teacher/supervision 如何提供、SFT 与 OPD 如何混合、是否有防止模型自我强化错误的机制。预训练研究者可以把这篇报告当作“区域化 continued pretraining + online post-training”的工程案例来读，重点不在单个 benchmark 分数，而在阶段设计和数据选择。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The report addresses how to inject strong regional language capability into an existing foundation model, with a focus on Southeast Asian languages. Instead of training a new foundation model from scratch, it adapts NVIDIA Nemotron 3 at two sparse-activation scales, 30B-A3B and 120B-A12B, through continued pretraining and post-training. The goal is to improve linguistic, cultural, and task coverage for Southeast Asian languages while preserving the general reasoning, coding, multilingual, and long-context capabilities of the base models.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is the regional continued-pretraining recipe, not a new architecture. The report gives a concrete case where a targeted 150B-token adaptation corpus, followed by post-training, yields consistent gains for lower-resource and mid-resource language groups. The effect holds at both 30B-A3B and 120B-A12B sparse-activation scales. A useful design choice is that the release separates continued-pretrained base checkpoints from post-trained checkpoints. This makes it easier to study what comes from foundation-level language and knowledge adaptation versus what comes from instruction and interaction tuning.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"Read the model and training sections carefully, especially the relationship among the four checkpoints: 30B-A3B-Base, 30B-A3B, 120B-A12B-Base, and 120B-A12B. This structure is useful because it separates continued pretraining from post-training. Then inspect the data section for the 150B-token mixture: language proportions, quality filtering, and the balance among QA, reasoning, code, and translation data are the most important details if available in the full report or appendices. The SEA-HELM section is worth reading by language and by capability, especially for Tamil and Burmese, where the report claims larger gains. Its reliance on derivatives limits its use when gradients are unavailable or unreliable. We develop a derivative-free framework that constructs Muon-style updates from structured finite differences. Four variants are considered: full entrywise recovery, random low-rank surrogates, basis-aligned rank-one probing, and direct structured search. Exhaustive basis-aligned probing is equivalent, up","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-15T19:08:19Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2609_17759.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["math.OC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论一个相对窄但有意思的问题：当梯度不可得或不可靠时，能否用函数值查询构造 Muon 风格的矩阵更新方向。Muon 的关键不是直接用梯度，而是对带动量的矩阵做极分解或近似正交化；论文关注的是如何用有限差分和结构化探测来替代梯度矩阵。","importance":"对 LLM 预训练的直接价值有限，但对理解 Muon 的优化几何有启发。Muon 已经被用于较大规模语言模型训练，这篇工作提醒一个细节：Muon 的 polar factor 会抹掉奇异值大小，只保留左右奇异方向，因此低秩梯度近似的误差不能简单用 Frobenius norm 判断。一个很小的低秩误差，经过极分解后可能变成单位权重方向。这对研究 Muon、Newton-Muon、低秩梯度压缩和结构化优化器都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"论文把函数值测量写成 rank-one 扰动形式：对矩阵参数 W 施加 H=uv^T 的扰动，用有限差分估计方向导数。然后用这些测量构造 Muon 所需的矩阵方向。它讨论了四类方法：第一是完整 entrywise recovery，相当于逐坐标有限差分恢复梯度矩阵，查询成本随 mn 增长；第二是 random low-rank surrogate，用随机 rank-one probe 得到低秩代理，减少函数查询但带来采样误差；第三是 basis-aligned rank-one probing，用坐标基上的 rank-one 探测恢复结构化信息，穷举时与坐标有限差分等价，只差极分解前的正比例缩放；第四是 direct structured search，不显式恢复完整梯度，而是在结构化 rank-one 方向上直接搜索可用更新。与常规 derivative-free optimization 不同，论文不是只拟合局部标量模型，而是围绕 Muon 的矩阵正交化更新来设计测量。","experiments":"论文给出的主要实验包括矩阵回归、神经网络训练和 CartPole 策略优化。矩阵回归实验显示，随机 rank-one probing 可以显著减少函数值查询数量，但得到的 Muon 更新不如完整或坐标探测准确。文中用一个 4×4 masked quadratic 例子说明查询节省：变量有 16 个元素，但只有两个位置有一阶信息；朴素中心差分需要 2mn=32 次函数评估，如果已知 mask，只需针对两个活跃坐标做 4 次评估。该例中初始 loss 为 5，理想 Muon 更新后降到 2。这个例子也说明 Muon 会把梯度奇异值 3 和 1 统一成单位权重，只保留对应行列方向。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 分钟。重点看算法部分中四种 derivative-free Muon 构造方式，以及 basis-aligned probing 与坐标有限差分的等价关系。实验部分主要看 query cost 与 update quality 的权衡，不必把它当作可直接用于大规模 LLM 训练的 recipe。更值得深挖的是 polar factor 对低秩近似误差的放大效应，这一点对分析 Muon 类优化器更有用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a narrow but technically relevant question: how to construct Muon-style matrix updates when gradients are unavailable or unreliable. Muon does not simply apply the raw gradient; it forms a momentum matrix and then approximately orthogonalizes it. The paper asks whether structured finite-difference measurements can provide a useful substitute for that matrix.","importance":"Its direct impact on large-scale LLM pretraining is limited, but it is useful for understanding the geometry of Muon-like optimizers. Muon has been used in multi-billion-parameter language model training, and this work highlights an important caveat: the polar factor removes singular values and keeps singular directions. Therefore, a low-rank gradient surrogate that looks accurate in a standard matrix norm may still induce a poor orthogonalized update if small singular directions are wrong.","technical_approach":"The method uses rank-one perturbations H=uv^T and finite-difference function evaluations to estimate directional information for a matrix parameter W. The paper studies four variants. Full entrywise recovery reconstructs the gradient by coordinate finite differences and costs O(mn) measurements. Random low-rank surrogates use random rank-one probes to reduce the number of queries, at the cost of noisier update directions. Basis-aligned rank-one probing uses coordinate-aligned probes; exhaustive probing is equivalent to coordinate finite differences up to positive scaling before ideal polar orthogonalization. Direct structured search avoids full gradient reconstruction and searches directly over structured rank-one update directions. The distinction from generic derivative-free optimization is that the target object is not only a scalar local model, but the orthogonalized Muon update.","experiments":"The experiments cover matrix regression, neural-network training, and CartPole policy optimization. The matrix-regression results show the main trade-off: random rank-one probing can substantially reduce function evaluations, but the resulting Muon update is less accurate than fuller reconstruction. The paper also gives a concrete 4×4 masked quadratic example. Although W has 16 entries, only two entries carry first-order information. Naive central differences require 2mn=32 function evaluations, while using the known mask requires only 4 evaluations. In this example, the initial objective decreases from 5 to 2 after the ideal Muon step. The example also illustrates that Muon maps singular values 3 and 1 to equal-weight singular directions.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 minutes. Focus on the algorithmic definitions of the four derivative-free Muon variants and the equivalence between exhaustive basis-aligned probing and coordinate finite differences. The most useful part for optimizer research is the discussion of how polar orthogonalization changes the meaning of approximation error. The experiments are best read as controlled evidence about query cost versus update quality, not as a practical recipe for LLM-scale training.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"探讨了 Muon 优化器的无导数变体，虽然主要针对小规模问题，但对理解 Muon 机制和探索非梯度优化有一定参考价值，可快速浏览方法部分。","en":"Explores derivative-free variants of the Muon optimizer. of repeatedly placing that data in the prompt, retrieval context, or an external memory system. The target is an architecture whose weights can be generated and adapted from user-provided facts, corrections, demonstrations, and outcomes during use.","importance":"Its value is conceptual rather than empirical. It reframes the next source of useful data as live interaction data, and it argues that some of this data should move from the context window into generated or adapted weights. This connects static pretraining data limits, MoE-style dynamic weights, hypernetwork-generated adapters, low-dimensional task adaptation, personalization, and continual learning. The proposal is relevant to pretraining researchers because it shifts part of the pretrain/post-train boundary into inference-time weight dynamics. The open issues are substantial: credit assignment, forgetting, safety, user isolation, rollback, serving cost, and evaluation.","technical_approach":"The paper first interprets MoE as a dynamic-weight network. For a token x, an MoE layer applies an effective weight W_eff(x)=sum_i g_i(x)W_i, so the active computation depends on the input. The authors argue that frontier MoE models still have two limitations for live learning: all experts must remain resident, and the expert bank is fixed after training. The proposed direction keeps input-conditioned weights but replaces the static expert bank with a compact generator or hypernetwork that produces weights, adapters, or low-dimensional codes from live data. Facts, instructions, demonstrations, and corrections that would normally be kept in context can instead be compiled into parameter states. The paper is careful to distinguish this from increasing long-term storage capacity; the generator is still bounded by its own parameters. The claimed benefit is amortization: the model no longer has to reread the same runtime data token by token, the context window is freed, and useful state can persist across turns.","experiments":"The provided content does not include experiments, model sizes, datasets, training curves, or quantitative comparisons. It cites prior work to support several premises: capability scaling with data and compute, limits of public human-generated text, hypernetworks as encoders for injected knowledge, and the low intrinsic dimension of task adaptation. The missing empirical tests are clear: comparisons against long-context prompting, RAG, and online LoRA-style updates under equal compute; retention and interference after weight generation; robustness to conflicting or false user data; user-level isolation; rollback mechanisms; and serving overhead under many concurrent personalized states.","reading_guide":"A 20-minute skim is sufficient. We ask whether the immediately preceding selection adequately summarizes this trajectory for predicting the next router. Using frozen OLMoE and JetMoE models, we measure the held-out predictive gain from earlier expert selections while retaining the most recent selection as a common baseline. In OLMoE, extending the history from one to eleven layers raises router-logit $R^2$ from 0.59879 to 0.66544. A prer","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-15T23:57:34Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_17940.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究一个很具体但对 MoE 机制很关键的问题：在稀疏 MoE 中，预测某一层 router 的选择时，前一层 expert selection 是否已经足够，还是更早层的 routing history 仍然包含额外信息。换句话说，它在测量 routing 是否近似一阶 Markov，还是存在跨多层的残余结构。这个问题重要，因为很多 MoE 设计默认局部路由状态已经足够，而如果更深历史仍有可预测信号，就说明当前 router 只看本层输入或上一层状态可能丢掉了可利用的路径信息。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在机制理解和架构启发，而不是直接给出新的训练 recipe。论文给出的证据支持这样一个判断：MoE 的 expert path 不是逐层独立的局部现象，而是沿深度形成了可持续的轨迹结构。若这一点成立，就会影响 router 设计、负载均衡策略、路径缓存、history-aware routing，甚至 MoE 的可解释性分析。它也给已有工作如 recurrent routing state、shared router、path replay 一类方法提供了更明确的经验依据：这些方法不是凭直觉加历史，而是在预训练好的 MoE 中确实存在“上一层之外”的剩余可预测结构。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者没有改模型训练，而是在冻结的预训练 MoE 上做 probing。核心设定很干净：固定目标层 l，用前 k 层的 expert selection 二值向量 S_{l-k:l-1} 去预测该层 router logits g_l，并把只用前一层 S_{l-1} 的预测作为共同基线。这样定义的增益 Δ_l(k) 直接回答“在已经给定最近一层 selection 的情况下，更早历史还能带来多少额外可预测性”。论文先做了几步铺垫。EIPC 比较不同历史表征：expert 输出融合后的向量、保留 expert identity slot 的 provenance 表征、以及打乱 identity 的版本，结论是保留 expert 身份的历史明显更有预测力。EPD 进一步把 provenance 拆成 path identity 与 contribution content，发现真正有用的主要是 selection path，而不是 expert 输出内容；在统一压缩预算下，path 单独就能达到很高的预测性能，而 content 几乎没有贡献。基于这个观察，RMO 实验专门测试 selection history beyond previous layer。为避免“只是输入维度更大”或“线性 probe 解码能力不够”的质疑，作者做了几类控制：一是对 recent state 和 older history 分别做 PCA，并让 baseline 也占用相同输入维度，只是把 older block 置零；二是在 JetMoE 上做 preregistered replication；三是用小 MLP 替代 ridge probe，并加入参数量匹配的 recent-only MLP；四是做 residual prediction，先用 recent state 分别预测目标 logits 和 older history，再看 history residual 是否还能预测 logit residual。这个 residual 设计很关键，因为它在问：即便给 recent state 一个非线性解码器，older history 是否仍含有 recent state 未解释掉的剩余信息。","experiments":"实验基于两个冻结 backbone：OLMoE-1B-7B-0125 和 jetmoe-8b，均用 WikiText-103-raw-v1 抽样 token 做 probe 训练与测试。OLMoE 是 64 个 expert 里选 8，JetMoE 是 8 选 2。主要数字有几组。第一，EIPC 中，保留 expert identity 的 provenance 表征相比简单 fusion，R^2 分别提升 0.10923 和 0.17506，平均增益 0.14215，说明‘谁被选中’比‘输出混合后是什么’更重要。第二，EPD 中，在 32 维压缩预算下，path alone 的预测性能达到 R^2=0.66969，而 content 只有 0.06732；把 content 加到 path 上反而只有 0.66728，几乎不增益，说明 selection identity 是主要可访问信号。第三，核心 RMO 结果：在 OLMoE 中，把历史从 1 层扩展到 11 层，预测 Layer 12 router logits 的 R^2 从 0.59879 提升到 0.66544，增益约 0.06665。第四，JetMoE 的 preregistered replication 在两个目标深度上，4 层历史相对 recent-only baseline 的增益分别为 0.14275 和 0.20528，且 paired bootstrap 区间都大于 0。第五，非线性解码控制中，给小型 MLP 加入 older history 后，R^2 还能提升 0.17137 和 0.21861；而参数匹配的 recent-only MLP 不能复现这一增益。第六，residual prediction 仍能从 history residual 预测 router residual，说明历史效应不是线性 probe 太弱造成的假象。整体上，实验链条比较完整：先确认什么历史表征有用，再确认 useful signal 主要来自 path，再确认 beyond-previous-layer 的增益在不同模型和非线性控制下都存在。","reading_guide":"这篇适合从‘MoE router 到底记住了什么’这个角度读。先看 Introduction 和 Measuring Historical Value，弄清楚作者的因变量不是最终 expert id，而是 router logits；这比直接预测 top-k 选择更细，因为它保留了路由偏好的连续结构。然后重点看 EIPC 和 EPD：这两部分决定了后面为什么只研究 selection path，而不继续纠缠 expert output content。接着看 RMO 主实验和 JetMoE replication，关注作者如何构造公平基线，避免把“更多历史”偷换成“更多参数/更高维输入”。如果你关心机制分析，最值得细看的是 nonlinear decoder 和 residual prediction 两节，它们直接回答‘历史增益是否只是 probe 太弱’。全文不长，泛读 20–30 分钟足够；若你在做 history-aware router、shared routing state 或 MoE interpretability，建议把实验协议和附录里的 probe 细节也看完。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise mechanistic question about sparse MoE routing: when predicting the router at layer l, is the immediately previous expert selection enough, or do older routing decisions still carry predictive information? In other words, is routing approximately first-order Markov across depth, or does expert-path history retain residual structure beyond the last layer? This matters because many MoE designs implicitly assume that local state is sufficient. If older routing history remains informative after conditioning on the most recent selection, then current routers may be discarding usable path information.","importance":"Its contribution is mainly to MoE understanding and architecture design rather than to training recipes. The evidence suggests that expert routing in pretrained sparse MoEs is not just a sequence of local, layer-by-layer decisions. It forms a deeper trajectory with persistent structure across depth. That has implications for router design, load-balancing strategies, history-aware routing, path caching, and interpretability. It also gives empirical support to ideas such as recurrent routing state, shared routers across blocks, and path replay: these are not merely heuristic additions, because pretrained MoEs already exhibit predictive structure beyond the previous layer.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"$1c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"揭示了 MoE 路由中存在跨层依赖，暗示当前逐层独立的路由设计可能次优，对设计新型路由机制（如考虑历史轨迹）有启发。","en":"Reveals cross-layer dependencies in MoE routing, suggesting that current layer-wise independent routing designs might be suboptimal, inspiring novel routing mechanisms that consider historical trajectories."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hao Li","Yasuyuki Tahara","Yuichi Sei"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["MoE","Routing Mechanism","Interpretability","Residual Structure"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17940","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.17940","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.18966","title":"The Automaton Underneath: The Additive Input Pathway Is a Parasitic Attractor for State Tracking in Householder Linear RNN","abstract":"Linear RNNs with input-dependent Householder-product transitions (DeltaNet/DeltaProduct-class) can provably represent hard state-tracking automata, yet trained models fail to length-generalize -- a gap recent work attributes to optimization, without a causal account. We give one, in a pre-registered, within-architecture causal ablation: the same model with one term deleted -- the additive input injection $b_t = W_b e_t$. With $b_t$, models fit length 32 and collapse out-of-distribution on parity","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-16T02:52:00Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_18966.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.NE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"线性 RNN（如 DeltaNet 变体）在理论上能精确表示复杂状态机，但在实际训练中却无法泛化到长序列，本文试图通过因果消融实验找出导致这种“表达能力与可学习性”鸿沟的根本原因。","importance":"通过严格的预注册实验，首次从因果层面证明了线性 RNN 中常见的加性输入注入项（additive input injection）是导致长序列泛化失败的“寄生吸引子”，为理解和改进线性 RNN 的训练动态提供了关键洞察。","technical_approach":"在固定的 DeltaProduct 风格的 Householder 线性 RNN 中，进行因果消融实验：对比包含加性输入注入项 $b_t = W_b e_t$（$+b$）和移除该项（$-b$）的模型。由于正交转移矩阵本身已具备足够的表达能力，移除 $b_t$ 不改变模型的理论表达上限，仅改变 SGD 的优化轨迹。实验在多种状态机任务（如奇偶校验、$S_4$、$A_5$、$S_5$）上进行。","experiments":"实验结果显示了惊人的反转：$+b$ 模型在长度 32 的训练集上完美拟合，但在长序列上崩溃（捷径解）；而 $-b$ 模型在相同配置下，成功学习到了精确的状态机，并完美泛化到长度 512 的序列（包括不可解的 $S_5$ 群）。进一步发现，在最小宽度下，精确解实际上隐藏在加性路径之下，推理时直接将 $W_b$ 置零（无需重新训练）即可在大部分种子中恢复精确泛化。","reading_guide":"重点关注第 3 节的消融实验结果和第 5 节的机制分析。特别是推理时置零 $W_b$ 恢复泛化的发现，极具启发性。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Linear RNNs (e.g., DeltaNet variants) can theoretically represent complex state machines but fail to length-generalize in practice. This paper seeks the causal root of this 'expressivity vs. learnability' gap through ablation.","importance":"Through rigorous pre-registered experiments, it provides the first causal proof that the standard additive input injection in linear RNNs acts as a 'parasitic attractor' causing length-generalization failure, offering crucial insights for improving linear RNN training dynamics.","technical_approach":"The authors perform a causal ablation on a fixed DeltaProduct-style Householder linear RNN, comparing models with ($+b$) and without ($-b$) the additive input injection $b_t = W_b e_t$. Since the orthogonal transitions alone are expressive enough, removing $b_t$ only alters the SGD optimization trajectory, not the theoretical capacity. Experiments span various state-tracking tasks (parity, $S_4$, $A_5$, $S_5$).","experiments":"Results show a stark reversal: $+b$ models fit length 32 perfectly but collapse on longer sequences (shortcut solution), whereas $-b$ models learn the exact automaton and generalize perfectly to length 512 (including the non-solvable $S_5$) under identical settings. Sparse attention only fetches the top-k K/V rows, but it still has to scan a cheap representation of every key to rank candidates before the fetch. This changes the practical assumptions behind million-token contexts and agentic sessions.","technical_approach":"The main idea is per-query, per-channel variable-depth key reads. Existing methods such as Loki, Double Sparsity, and SparQ read a fixed set of coordinates or a fixed number of bits. Fathom stores the 4-bit key cache in channel-major bit planes. With uniform symmetric quantization, the first t bit planes of a channel are exactly the t-bit quantizer under the same block scale, so prefix reads are valid lower-precision reads without maintaining separate thumbnail indices. The bit allocation follows reverse water-filling. If channel j has query-dependent importance g_j, then the marginal reduction in score error from one more bit after t_j bits is proportional to g_j 4^{-t_j}. This favors early bits of important channels, but avoids spending late bits where a first bit on another channel is more useful. The method also includes an optional per-layer budget calibrated once. For basis choice, it uses raw channels for QK-normalized models such as Qwen3, and KLT planes for models such as Llama-3.1 and Qwen2.5.","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"A focused read should take about 25 to 35 minutes. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T00:04:13Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_17943.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长上下文解码的主要瓶颈是反复读取每个请求独立的 长度差异很大，同一请求在生成过程中也会变化。","importance":"这篇工作对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对长上下文模型的 serving 研究有参考价值。它指出 speculative decoding 在长上下文场景下的核心问题不只是 draft 模型或稀疏注意力质量，而是 batch 内请求的异步性。对基座研究员的启发在于：长上下文能力能否实际落地，很大程度取决于 decode batch 统一执行 draft 阶段或 verify 阶段。第二，使用在线 speculation scheduler，为每个请求维护 acceptance rate 估计，并结合当前 batch 下 draft 与 verify 的成本比，动态决定该请求下一步继续 draft 还是进入 verify。第三，引入 intra-draft context refresh：draft 仍使用 partial KV 或稀疏注意力以减少内存流量，但在 draft 过程中选择一个 refresh layer 做全注意力，利用相邻 token 与跨层注意力模式的相关性更新 partial KV，使后续 draft 的上下文不至于过期。相比 MagicDec 的 window attention 和 Vegas 的基于 verification attention score KV 会限制 draft 质量，轻量刷新能支持更长、更有效的 draft。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 25 到 35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 attention memory-bound 的拆解，以及 empirical study 中关于 optimal draft length 异质性的图表。方法部分优先看 unified mixed forward 和 scheduler，它们决定了这篇工作的系统价值。context refresh 可以作为第二重点，关注它如何在额外 full attention 开销和 draft 质量之间取平衡。实验部分看吞吐、延迟、batch size、context length 维度下的曲线，比单个最大加速数字更重要。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Long-context decoding is dominated by repeated KV-cache reads, and batching does not amortize those reads because each request owns a separate cache. Existing batched self-speculative decoding systems reduce full-attention calls by drafting with sparse attention and verifying with full attention, but they still synchronize all requests in a batch under one draft-verify schedule. That is a poor fit for real workloads, where the best draft length varies across requests and changes during a single generation.","importance":"The paper is mainly an inference-systems contribution, not a pretraining contribution. Its relevance to foundation-model research is practical: long-context capability is constrained not only by model architecture and training data, but also by the memory traffic pattern during decoding. The useful signal is that speculative decoding should be treated as a per-request scheduling problem under batch-level hardware costs, rather than only as a better drafter design problem.","technical_approach":"ASPIRE introduces a non-synchronized batched self-speculative decoding framework. Its first component is a unified mixed forward pass, where some requests in the same batch can draft using partial KV context while others verify using full KV context. This removes global draft and verify phases. The second component is a lightweight online scheduler. It tracks per-request acceptance behavior and uses a batch-aware draft-to-verify cost ratio to decide whether each request should keep drafting or verify in the next model forward. The third component is an intra-draft refresh mechanism. During drafting, a selected refresh layer performs full attention and updates the partial KV set for the next token, exploiting the correlation of attention patterns across layers and neighboring tokens. Compared with MagicDec and Vegas, the main shift is from synchronized batch-level speculation to per-request asynchronous speculation, with an additional mechanism to reduce stale-context effects during long drafts.","experiments":"The paper evaluates ASPIRE on three models and five workloads. It reports up to 4.58x throughput improvement over autoregressive decoding and consistently stronger overall performance than prior self-speculative decoding baselines such as MagicDec and Vegas. The most important empirical finding is that optimal draft lengths are highly heterogeneous across requests and dynamic within each request. This explains why synchronized batched speculation leaves performance on the table. Ablations around context refresh show that a stale partial KV context degrades draft quality, while a lightweight refresh can extend useful draft lengths with limited overhead.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25 to 35 minutes. Start with the motivation around attention being memory-bound in long-context decoding. Then focus on the empirical study of optimal draft-length variability, since that is the main reason the system design is needed. In the method section, read the unified mixed forward and online scheduler carefully. The refresh-layer design is worth a secondary pass, especially the cost-quality tradeoff. In the experiments, prioritize throughput and latency curves under different batch sizes and context lengths over the single best speedup number.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看调度模型和 mixed forward 设计；长上下文服务的瓶颈判断干净，但最终价值取决于真实 batch 分布。","en":"Read the scheduler and mixed-forward design; the bottleneck framing is clean, but the payoff depends heavily on real batch heterogeneity."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Southern California"],"authors":["Amir Ziashahabi","Hossein Entezari Zarch","Lei Gao","Murali Annavaram","Salman Avestimehr"],"notable_authors":["Salman Avestimehr","Murali Annavaram"],"tags":["speculative-decoding","long-context","KV-cache","serving"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17943","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.17943","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.17983","title":"Contiguity, Not Importance: Budgeted Repair of Stale KV Caches After Document Edits","abstract":"KV-cache reuse can reduce inference cost in retrieval-augmented generation and agentic systems, but cached contexts may become stale when retrieved knowledge, working memory, or user state is edited. S，再在下游候选区间中选择 K 个位置重算。重算按层进行，未选位置保留旧 KV。比较的训练无关策略包括：编辑点附近的连续窗口 EditLocal、结构分隔符位置、基于 stale-query attention 的选择、基于 CacheBlend KV deviation 的选择、随机选择。还加入了 transplant-derived causal ranking 作为诊断，但它不是可部署策略，因为它使用了 clean cache 状态。方法设计的重点是把“从干净状态移植哪些位置有效”和“在旧上下文包围下实际重算哪些位置有效”分开。","experiments":"实验使用约 5000 token 的检索上下文，构造 paired factual edits。direct 问题答案在编辑 span 内，derived answer margin，flip rate 为 0.95–1.00，比其他策略高 0.46–1.00 margin recovery，Holm 校正后 p ≤ 3×10^-9。邻接性很关键：答案文本移到下游 250 token 后，EditLocal recovery 降到 0.01–0.09，stale-query attention 只在一个模型家族上有帮助。transplant ρ=0.17。repair 比 full re-prefill 快 13–21 倍，因此在该设定下无条件执行 EditLocal repair 是更稳的系统策略。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。先看问题形式化，尤其是 length-preserving edit、已知编辑 span、下游预算 K 的设定边界。然后看 selector 对比和 direct/derived benchmark 构造。实验中最值得看的图表是 K=32 下不同策略的 margin recovery 和 flip rate、250-token distance-controlled 变体、以及 transplant ranking 与 recomputation 的对照。需要注意适用范围：结论主要支持长度不变、编辑点已知、factual RAG、约 5k memory blocks. In RAG and agent systems, cached KV states save prefill cost, but an edited source document makes the cache inconsistent with the new text. Under causal self-attention, even a small local edit can affect all downstream states. The core question is which downstream positions should be recomputed under a small repair budget.","importance":"This is mainly a serving and long-context systems paper, but it has useful implications for cache-aware model deployment. Its main lesson is that, for length-preserving factual edits, repair quality is driven by rebuilding a contiguous dependency chain near the edit, not by selecting isolated positions that look important under a separate diagnostic. This matters for RAG cache reuse, agent memory updates, and any system that treats KV states as reusable intermediate representations.","technical_approach":"The paper formulates in-place cache repair as budgeted recomputation. Given an original context C and a length-preserving edited context C', the known edit span S is always refreshed. A policy then selects K downstream positions for recomputation, while unselected KV entries remain stale. The compared training-free selectors include a contiguous edit-local window, structural delimiters, stale-query attention, CacheBlend-style KV deviation, and random selection. A transplant-derived causal ranking is included only as a diagnostic. The important distinction is between positions that are useful when clean states are transplanted and positions that are useful when recomputed while surrounded by stale states.","experiments":"$1e","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is sufficient. Read the formal setup first, especially the assumptions of length-preserving edits, known edit spans, and a fixed downstream recomputation budget. Then inspect the construction of direct and derived edit pairs. The key experimental sections are the K=32 selector comparison, the 250-token distance-controlled variant, and the transplant-versus-recomputation analysis. The main limitation to keep in mind is scope: the evidence supports length-preserving factual edits with known locations in about 5k-token RAG contexts. Length-changing edits, composed caches, and multi-step agent memory updates need separate validation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看方法和 derived-edit 实验即可；它提醒 KV cache 修复不能迷信 attention importance，连续重算可能是更稳的默认策略。","en":"Read the method and derived-edit results; it is a useful warning that attention-importance selection is a shaky default for stale KV repair."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of South Florida","DEVCOM Army Research Laboratory","University of South Florida, Electrical and Computer Engineering"],"authors":["Mingyang Mao","Wyatt Mackey","Xiaomin Lin"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["kv-cache","rag","inference-efficiency","cache-consistency"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17983","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.17983","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.18063","title":"The Other Half of the Memory Wall: Serving 35B MoEs from SSD with Trained Routing Prediction","abstract":"Mixture-of-experts (MoE) inference on consumer hardware is bounded by weight memory: a 35B-class model is 19.5GB at 4-bit, and sparsity shrinks the compute per token, not the bytes that must be held. paper addresses a practical but under-discussed deployment bottleneck for large MoEs: sparsity reduces compute per token, but it does not remove the need to store tens of gigabytes of expert weights. Simply offloading experts to SSD is not enough, because the experts needed for layer N+1 are only known after layer N finishes for the current token. That creates an I/O stall at every layer and every decode step. The central question is not further model compression, but how to make a non-resident MoE decode as a streaming system.","importance":"Its main relevance is at the interface between architecture and serving. Serving systems decide whether that cache stays, leaves, or comes back by guessing how long the tool will run, from the tool's name, its history, a duration declared before the call, or the engine's own occupancy. We show that no estimate fixed before a call starts can know its duration, and such estimates may not even rank the calls. Meanwhile, the running tool already agent 和推理服务有启发。关键点在于：agent 的有效能力越来越受 serving 侧显存管理约束，特别是 KV cache、工具等待、并发请求之间的竞争。论文把调度信号从“调用前预测”改成“运行中报告进度”，这是一个更接近系统真实状态的接口设计。对基座研究员的价值主要在于理解 posttrain/agent 能力落地时，模型上下文、工具链和 serving 系统之间的耦合。","technical_approach":"方法不是训练模型，而是改 agent 工具调用与 serving engine 的接口。作者先证明调用前估计存在结构性限制：长调用由机器负载、sandbox 邻居、远端 API 状态等运行时因素决定，静态特征和历史数据很难泛化。然后提出让工具调用在运行中显式报告进度。进度信号分两档：一类能估计剩余工作量，另一类只能提示结束接近。作者构建了一个 harness，在不改变 agent 可见输出的前提下读取工具进度。具体做法包括解除多层静默、把 stdout/stderr 与环境事件合并成事件流、从进度条、计数器、构建日志、测试运行等信号中恢复进度。服务端增加少量调度 primitive，用这些进度信号决定 KV cache 是否继续留在 HBM、换到 host memory，或在工具结束前拉回。论文也讨论了进度上报不可信的问题，用 session-credit 机制限制恶意或错误报告能带来的资源收益。","experiments":"实验覆盖四个公开 agent 语料，重点分析 coding agent 的工具调用。一个核心观察是等待时间高度集中：在 p90 TTFT 降低 20.7%（仅 HBM）和 20.8%（HBM + DRAM），接近 oracle 策略。结果说明主要收益来自长尾工具调用的 KV cache 决策，而不是短调用。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。重点看 Section 2.1 的失败案例和 Figures 2–3，理解为什么调用前预测不能解决长调用排序问题；看 Section 2.2、Table 2、Figures 4–5，了解工具进度信号的分类和可恢复比例；看 Sections 3–4 的 harness 设计和预测器对比；最后看 Section 5 与 Figure 9，关注 serving primitive、HBM/DRAM 分层结果和 session-credit 防滥用机制。若只关心 LLM serving，可以跳过部分语料细节，保留接口设计和调度结果。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a narrow but real serving problem in agentic workloads: while an LLM waits for a tool call, its KV cache occupies GPU memory, yet the serving system has to decide whether to keep, evict, or prefetch that cache using only pre-call guesses. The authors argue that long tool-call duration is largely determined at runtime, so command names, historical durations, declared durations, and engine occupancy are weak signals for ranking the calls that matter.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the deployment boundary of long-context and tool-using models. The useful idea is to replace pre-call duration prediction with an explicit runtime progress channel from tools to the serving system. As agent workloads spend substantial wall-clock time outside the model, effective model capability becomes constrained by KV-cache residency, memory tiers, and scheduling. The paper makes that interface concrete and measurable.","technical_approach":"The proposed mechanism changes the interface between tools, the agent harness, and the serving engine. Instead of predicting duration before the call starts, tools report progress while running and revise that report over time. The signal comes in two strengths: estimates of remaining work, or weaker indications that completion is near. The authors build a harness that recovers these signals without changing what the agent observes. It removes several layers of suppressed output, merges output and environment events into one stream, and extracts progress from counters, progress bars, build logs, test runners, and similar runtime traces. The serving engine then uses this signal for KV-cache residency and prefetch decisions across HBM and host memory. Since reported progress is untrusted input, the design adds a session-credit mechanism to bound the benefit of lying sessions.","experiments":"The empirical study uses four public agent corpora. In mini-SWE-agent traces on SWE-bench leaderboard runs, fewer than 3% of tool calls account for almost two thirds of total tool time, mainly installs, builds, tests, and generated scripts. The recovered progress signal is compared against four classes of existing predictors at the points where cache decisions are made, including under changing environments. In end-to-end GPU serving experiments, progress-aware scheduling reduces post-tool-call p90 TTFT by 20.7% with an HBM-only tier and 20.8% with HBM plus DRAM, close to an oracle policy. The gains come from making better decisions on the small number of long calls; short calls are mostly insensitive to the policy.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Read Section 2.1 and Figures 2–3 for the evidence that pre-call estimators fail on long-call ranking. Read Section 2.2, Table 2, and Figures 4–5 for the taxonomy of progress signals and how much tool time exposes usable progress. Sections 3–4 are the main systems contribution: the harness and the comparison against duration predictors. Section 5 and Figure 9 are the most relevant parts for serving researchers, covering the engine primitives, HBM/DRAM results, and the session-credit safeguard.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"非常务实的 Serving 优化思路。与其让 vLLM 盲猜 Tool Call 要多久从而决定是否 swap KV cache，不如让 Tool 直接吐出进度。做 Agent 基础设施的同学必看。","en":"A very pragmatic Serving optimization approach. Instead of having vLLM blindly guess how long a Tool Call will take to decide whether to swap the KV cache, it's better to let the Tool emit progress directly. Expert pruning is the most direct approach for reducing this parameter count, yet existing methods make pruning decisions for each expert independently, and assume experts' contributions are purely additive. In reality, expert usage in MoEs is inherently cooperative. We derive HOPE (Higher-Order Pruning of Experts), a second-order pruning objective which provably minimizes a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T16:55:46Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_18916.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 MoE 模型的专家剪枝问题：现有方法通常给每个 expert 一个独立重要性分数，再按分数删除，但 MoE 的实际计算是多个 expert 被同一个 token 共同选中并加权组合。忽略 expert 之间的协同关系，在高剪枝率下容易破坏成组工作的 expert 结构。","importance":"对基座模型研究有直接价值，尤其是 MoE 模型的部署压缩和训练后结构分析。MoE 的参数主要集中在 expert MLP 上，即使每个 token 只激活少数 expert，部署时仍需要把完整 expert pool 放入显存。专家剪枝与量化互补，可以直接减少常驻参数，为 KV cache、长上下文、更多并发或更长 reasoning trace 留出显存。论文的启发点在于：MoE 压缩不能只看单个 expert 的边际贡献，还要看 expert 共现和组合结构。","technical_approach":"作者提出 HOPE，即 Higher-Order Pruning of Experts。设一个 MoE 层有 E 个 expert，router 对每个 token 选择 top-K expert 并用 gate weight 加权求和。删除某个 expert 后，原本会被选中的 expert 会被 next-highest-logit expert 替代，gate weight 也会重新归一化。HOPE 的目标是选择一个 prune-set，使剪枝前后层输出误差的上界尽量小。与一阶方法不同，HOPE 在校准阶段记录 expert 使用情况以及 expert pair 的交互项，为每一层构造一个 E×E 的交互矩阵，然后在给定剪枝预算下解一个二次规划。REAP 可以看作 HOPE 去掉 pairwise interaction 后的特例。这个设计的重点不是重新训练模型，而是用一次校准捕获 expert 之间的协同结构，避免在激进剪枝时同时破坏多个常共现的 expert 组合。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个 MoE 架构，最大到 122B 参数；使用 2 个校准集、6 个剪枝率，并在多个 benchmark 上评估。论文特别强调 Qwen3.5-122B-A10B 这类模型中 expert MLP 占绝大多数参数：122B 参数里约 116B 是 expert，约 240GB 显存占用；如果剪掉 50% expert，模型 footprint 可降到约 125GB，释放约 115GB。结果显示 HOPE 在多数 head-to-head 比较中优于已有强基线，优势在 40–50% 高剪枝率时更明显；复杂 agentic tasks 上收益更突出，符合作者关于 expert 协同结构的解释。分析实验也显示 HOPE 倾向保留具有更强合作关系的 expert。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–45 分钟。重点看方法部分对剪枝误差的定义：删除 expert 后由哪些替代 expert 接管，以及 gate 重新归一化如何改变层输出。随后看 Appendix A 的推导，确认二阶目标为什么是误差上界的近似或上界优化；再看与 REAP 的关系，这能帮助判断 HOPE 的真实增量。实验部分优先看不同剪枝率的曲线，特别是 40–50% 区间；再看复杂 agentic benchmark 与普通 benchmark 的差异。如果只关心部署，重点看显存释放数字、是否需要 retraining、校准成本和剪枝后是否仍能与量化组合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies expert pruning in MoE language models. Existing pruning methods usually score each expert independently and remove low-scoring experts, but MoE inference is cooperative: each token activates a group of experts whose outputs are combined. Removing experts independently can break frequently co-selected expert groups, especially at high pruning rates.","importance":"This is directly relevant to MoE model compression and deployment. Expert MLPs dominate the parameter count of large MoEs, and all experts usually have to remain resident in memory even though each token activates only a few of them. Pruning entire experts reduces the persistent memory footprint and composes with quantization. The more general lesson is that MoE structure should be analyzed at the level of expert combinations, not only individual expert importance.","technical_approach":"HOPE, Higher-Order Pruning of Experts, formulates pruning as a second-order objective. In an MoE layer, the router selects the top-K experts for each token and combines their outputs with normalized gate weights. When an expert is removed, the router backfills with the next-highest-logit expert and renormalizes the gates. HOPE chooses the prune set that minimizes an upper bound on the resulting layer-output error. During calibration, it records expert usage and pairwise interaction terms, building an E-by-E interaction matrix for each layer. The pruning problem is then solved as a quadratic program under a fixed budget. REAP is recovered as the special case where the interaction terms are ignored. The key design choice is to capture co-selection and complementarity among experts without retraining the model.","experiments":"The experiments cover three MoE architectures up to 122B parameters, two calibration sets, six pruning rates, and multiple benchmarks. The memory motivation is concrete: in Qwen3.5-122B-A10B, about 116B of 122B parameters are expert MLPs, using roughly 240GB; pruning 50% of experts would reduce the footprint to about 125GB and free around 115GB. HOPE wins against strong pruning baselines in most pairwise comparisons, with larger margins at 40–50% pruning. The advantage is most visible on complex agentic tasks, where diverse expert combinations appear to matter more. Additional analysis indicates that HOPE preferentially preserves experts with stronger cooperative structure.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–45 minutes. Focus first on the pruning-error setup: what happens when a selected expert is deleted, which replacement experts enter top-K, and how gate renormalization affects the layer output. Then read the derivation in Appendix A to understand the second-order upper-bound objective and the relation to REAP. In the experiments, prioritize pruning-rate curves, especially the 40–50% region, and compare ordinary benchmarks with agentic tasks. In this paper, we ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T04:33:08Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_18112.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决多租户 LLM serving 中的延迟隔离问题。现有公平调度多按长期吞吐量做公平，不能保证单个 token 的生成延迟。结果是一个高需求租户可以显著拉高其他租户的 TTFT、TBT 和 TTLT，即使后者的负载没有超过合理份额。","importance":"它属于 serving 系统论文，但对基座研究员仍有间接价值。预训练侧越来越需要理解模型结构、KV cache、长上下文和推理成本之间的关系；这篇论文把公平性定义从请求级或吞吐级推进到 token 级，更贴近自回归解码的真实瓶颈。它也说明，LLM 服务中的隔离问题不只是排队论问题，还受到 KV cache 驱逐、prefill/decode 资源差异、GPU 缺少细粒度资源划分机制等因素影响。","technical_approach":"论文提出 Token Latency Fairness，也称 δ-token fairness。定义是：对一个 well-behaved client，如果某个 token 在隔离执行下需要 d 个时间单位生成，那么在多租户共享执行下应在 d+δ 内生成。δ 是系统管理员配置的最大可接受额外延迟。这个定义与长期吞吐公平不同，它直接约束每个 token 的 latency spike。系统 FairInference 的核心是 δ-fair scheduler：先估计 token 在隔离执行或公平份额执行下的基准延迟，再把 δ 作为多租户执行中的额外 slack，用这个 token-level deadline 来调度。论文还强调 LLM serving 的特殊困难：prefill 是 compute-bound，decode 更偏 memory-bound；共享 KV cache 会导致跨租户驱逐，恢复状态需要重算或从二级存储取回；GPU 也缺少像 CPU 那样细粒度、可持续的资源分区机制。","experiments":"文中报告的关键现象是干扰幅度很大：在 SGLang 中，一个高需求 client 可以让另一个 client 的 TTFT 增加超过 60×，其他 token-level latency 指标也会恶化。已有公平 serving 方法如 VTC 和 DLPM 虽然追求长期吞吐公平，但仍可能让 well-behaved client 的 TTFT 增加一个数量级。论文围绕这些现象提出 δ-token fairness，并用 FairInference 作为实现。给定内容中没有完整实验表，因此不宜过度解读吞吐损失、尾延迟分位数或不同 workload 下的具体数值。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25 到 30 分钟。先看 fairness definition，明确 δ-token fairness 与吞吐公平、请求级 deadline、公平批处理之间的区别。然后看系统设计部分，重点关注 isolated latency estimation、token-level deadline scheduling 和 KV cache 管理。实验部分优先看高需求租户对 TTFT/TBT/TTLT 的干扰，以及 FairInference 在隔离保证和吞吐之间的取舍。对预训练研究而言，最有用的是它揭示了模型解码形态与服务公平性之间的耦合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses latency isolation in multi-tenant LLM serving. Existing fairness mechanisms mostly equalize throughput over time, but they do not bound the latency of individual generated tokens. As a result, a high-demand tenant can cause large spikes in TTFT, TBT, and TTLT for another tenant, even when that tenant stays within its fair resource share.","importance":"This is primarily a serving-systems paper, but it has useful implications for base-model researchers who care about decoding behavior, KV-cache pressure, long-context serving, and inference-aware model design. The key contribution is a fairness notion defined at token granularity, which matches the streaming nature of autoregressive generation better than request-level or long-term throughput fairness.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces Token Latency Fairness, or δ-token fairness. For a well-behaved client, if a token would be generated in d time units under isolated execution, then in multi-tenant execution it should be generated within d+δ time units. The parameter δ is the maximum tolerated additional delay. FairInference implements this idea with a δ-fair scheduler. The scheduler estimates the isolated or fair-share latency of each token, adds δ as the allowed slack, and schedules token generation to respect those token-level deadlines. The design is shaped by LLM-specific constraints: prefill is compute-bound, decode is more memory-bound, KV cache is shared across tenants, cache eviction can cause recomputation or slow retrieval, and GPUs do not provide fine-grained resource partitioning comparable to CPU scheduling.","experiments":"The paper reports severe interference in existing systems. In SGLang, one high-demand client can increase another client’s TTFT by more than 60×, with degradation in other latency metrics as well. Throughput-oriented fair serving systems such as VTC and DLPM can still increase a well-behaved client’s TTFT by up to an order of magnitude. The available text establishes these observations and motivates FairInference, but it does not provide the full experimental tables needed to make precise claims about throughput overhead, tail-latency percentiles, or workload-specific trade-offs.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 30 minutes. rat","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-15T22:30:48Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_17890.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是大推理模型 等同处理，导致剪枝保留的是统计显著的激活，而不一定是对正确推理有贡献的激活。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在训练新模型，而在提供了一种观察 RL 后推理模型内部计算的压缩视角。它提示：LRM 的参数重要性不能只由 activation magnitude 或 reconstruction error 决定，还要看这些激活是否参与了成功的 difficulty-matched 的正确/错误 CoT rollout 对，避免简单题的正确样本和难题的错误样本混在一起，使 outcome 差异更接近推理质量差异。第二，把 CoT 按句子切分，用 attention-suppression intervention 估计每个推理句子对最终答案的因果贡献。这个干预不需要在正确和错误轨迹之间做 token 对齐，适合长度和表达都不一致的 CoT。第三，把句子级贡献转成 token 权重，在校准 activations 中上调正确轨迹中有正贡献的 token，下调错误轨迹中相关 token，再接入 SparseGPT、Wanda、ALPS 等 one-shot 剪枝方法。和常规校准相比，它不是寻找“哪些权重在 CoT 中常被用到”，而是偏向保护“哪些权重支撑正确 outcome”。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个结构差异较大的剪枝后端：SparseGPT、Wanda 和 ALPS，用于验证方法不是绑定某个 solver 的技巧。论文重点比较 generic calibration、普通 self-generated reasoning calibration 和 outcome-based calibration。给出的主要现象是：只用通用语料校准时，LRM 在相同稀疏率下比普通指令模型掉点更明显；用自生成 CoT 可恢复部分性能，但仍会保留错误推理模式；加入 outcome 加权后，剪枝模型更接近 dense 模型的有效推理计算，准确率下降更小，同时减少剪枝后 CoT 变长和不终止的倾向。正文片段未提供完整 benchmark suppression sentence score、token re-weighting。实验部分重点看三类结果：不同剪枝后端是否一致受益；正确率和 CoT 长度是否同时改善；错误 rollout 被下调后是否真的减少无效推理模式。值得留意的细节是 attention suppression 的粒度选择。句子级干预比 token 级更稳定，也避免跨轨迹对齐，但可能会漏掉细粒度的关键 token。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets calibration mismatch in one-shot pruning for large reasoning models. Reasoning models rely on long chain-of-thought traces, while standard calibration corpora do not activate the reasoning-specific circuits induced by RL post-training. Prior reasoning-aware pruning uses the model’s own rollouts, but treats correct and incorrect traces as equally useful. This can preserve parameters that support frequent but wrong reasoning patterns.","importance":"The main value for pretraining-oriented research is diagnostic rather than a new pretraining recipe. It shows that parameter importance in RL-trained reasoning models should be conditioned on outcome, not only on activation magnitude or local reconstruction error. This matters for pruning, reasoning distillation, continual training, and data selection for reasoning models, because it separates paths the model often takes from paths that actually lead to correct answers.","technical_approach":"OBC-Prune changes the calibration statistics while leaving the pruning solver unchanged. It first builds difficulty-matched pairs of correct and incorrect chain-of-thought rollouts, so that correctness is less confounded with problem difficulty. It then scores reasoning sentences through an attention-suppression intervention, estimating how much each sentence causally affects the final outcome. This avoids token-level alignment across different rollouts. Finally, it converts sentence scores into per-token weights: activations from useful correct reasoning are upweighted, while activations associated with erroneous reasoning are downweighted. The weighted calibration data can be plugged into SparseGPT, Wanda, and ALPS without changing their optimization procedure.","experiments":"The experiments are designed around three pruning backends with different sensitivities to calibration quality: SparseGPT, Wanda, and ALPS. The reported pattern is that generic calibration hurts reasoning models more severely than conventional instruction models at comparable sparsity. Self-generated reasoning calibration recovers part of the loss, but still preserves erroneous reasoning traces. Outcome-weighted calibration improves accuracy retention and reduces the tendency of pruned models to produce longer or non-terminating chains of thought. The provided text does not include the full benchmark tables or exact sparsity-by-accuracy numbers, so the useful quantitative reading should focus on the paper’s tables for sparsity levels, backend-specific gains, and trace-length changes.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough. If the model initially complies, a second turn pushes again. The evaluation reports six behavioral axes rather than only one aggregate score: baseline compliance, resistance to pressure, resistance to multi-turn pushback, steerability through system prompts, transparency when violating rules, and rule-scope discernment. Each sample is repeated three times, and the model must satisfy the criteria in all repetitions to pass. Using this protocol, we create EDCT-Bench, a compr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T00:25:31Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_17953.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在生成自然语言解释（NLEs）时，经常出现“看似合理但与实际引用的视觉证据不一致”的忠诚度（faithfulness）差距。现有评估方法多依赖人类对解释合理性的主观判断，无法验证模型是否真正依赖其声称的视觉特征进行推理。","importance":"提出了一种基于反事实干预的自动化评估框架 EDCT，将 VLM 解释的忠诚度评估从“被动的人类主观打分”转变为“主动的因果行为测试”。这为理解 VLM 的内部推理机制和缓解幻觉提供了更严格的诊断工具，同时也为构建更高质量的对齐数据提供了新思路。","technical_approach":"EDCT 框架包含四个步骤：1) 基线响应获取与概念提取：让 VLM 回答问题并给出解释，用 LLM 从解释中提取具体的视觉概念；2) 反事实生成：使用文本引导的图像编辑器（如 FLUX.2）对提取的视觉概念进行最小化修改（添加、修改或删除）；3) 局部编辑验证：利用开放词汇检测器和分割模型生成编辑区域的掩码，通过像素级和结构级指标验证编辑是否局部且有效；4) 一致性测试：用修改后的图像重新查询 VLM，计算反事实一致性分数（CCS），判断模型的答案和解释是否随之发生合理变化。","experiments":"构建了 EDCT-Bench，包含 300 个经过验证的反事实测试用例，覆盖知识密集型 VQA、自动驾驶和 3D 空间推理三个领域。实验表明，所有评估的 VLM（包括强大的闭源模型）在反事实测试下均表现出显著的忠诚度差距。此外，EDCT 生成的反事实样本可作为困难的训练数据，微调后的模型在编辑区域的注意力分布更加集中。","reading_guide":"建议重点关注图 1（EDCT 框架流程）和算法 1（局部编辑验证机制）。可以深入思考这种基于干预的评估方法如何与现有的 VLM 预训练目标结合，以提升模型内在的跨模态对齐能力。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often exhibit a faithfulness gap when generating Natural Language Explanations (NLEs), producing plausible justifications that are inconsistent with the actual visual evidence they cite. Existing evaluation methods rely heavily on human judgments of plausibility, failing to verify if the model truly relies on the claimed visual features.","importance":"Introduces EDCT, an automated evaluation framework based on counterfactual interventions, shifting VLM explanation faithfulness evaluation from passive human scoring to active causal behavioral testing. This provides a more rigorous diagnostic tool for understanding VLM reasoning mechanisms and mitigating hallucinations, while also offering a novel approach for constructing higher-quality alignment data.","technical_approach":"The EDCT framework consists of four stages: 1) Baseline response acquisition and concept extraction: Prompt the VLM for an answer and explanation, then use an LLM to extract specific visual concepts from the explanation. 2) Counterfactual generation: Use text-guided image editors (e.g., FLUX.2) to apply minimal edits (add, modify, or remove) to the extracted concepts. 3) Localized edit verification: Generate a mask of the edited region using an open-vocabulary detector and segmentation model, verifying the edit's locality and effectiveness via pixel-level and structural metrics. 4) Consistency testing: Re-query the VLM with the edited image and calculate a Counterfactual Consistency Score (CCS) to determine if the model's answer and explanation change consistently.","experiments":"Constructed EDCT-Bench with 300 verified counterfactual tests across three domains: knowledge-intensive VQA, autonomous driving, and 3D spatial reasoning. Application-to-task factorization decomposes an interactive application into features at different granularities and organizes them through prerequisite lineages. Reference-to-current distillation asks an agent to observe a working reference application through browser interaction, then modify the current incomplete application so that the same behavior is recovered. The automated mine-craft-patch pipeline implements this process. The mine stage discovers reproducible browser-interaction traces. The craft stage masks the source implementation responsible for those behaviors and turns them into repair tasks. The patch stage evaluates agents by asking them to recover the missing behavior from the reference. Each trace acts as a behavioral verifier: it succeeds on the intact application, fails after masking, and is replayed after a submitted repair.","experiments":"The benchmark contains 4,063 tasks derived from 1,975 replay-verified behaviors across 26 interactive web applications. In full-application reconstruction, GPT-6 Astra recovers 49.2% of evaluated workflows, while Claude Opus 5 recovers 28.8%. In partial reconstruction, performance drops as restoration depth increases from 1 to 8: GPT-6 Astra falls from 100% to 64.0%, and Claude Opus 5 falls from 96% to 32%. Trajectory analysis shows that deeper tasks create a mismatch between reconstruction burden and agent effort, with observation effort per required behavior declining as depth grows. 最终是否完成任务”，而是更细一层的问题：长轨迹 agent 在哪里第一次出错、之后是否能自我发现并恢复、以及过程中是否已经造成不可逆伤害。作者指出，现有 agent 评测大多把整条轨迹压成 pass/fail，一个成功结果可能掩盖中途的破坏性操作，一个失败结果也无法区分是早期致命错误、后期局部失误，还是中途曾恢复但最终又偏离。论文因此把研究对象从 outcome-level evaluation 转到 trajectory-level failure localization，并且覆盖软件工程、computer use、AI-for-science 三类接近真实部署的长时程任务。","importance":"对基座模型研究的直接价值不在于提出新的预训练算法，而在于补上了长时程 agent 训练与评测里最缺的一环：高质量、可定位、可归因的失败信号。这样的信号可以反哺 reward modeling、process supervision、trajectory filtering、test-time selection 和安全审计。若论文中的结论成立，即“agent 在第一次错误后通常难以恢复，且很少能自行察觉错误”，那么只看最终成功率会系统性高估 agent 的可靠性，也会给后训练提供噪声很大的奖励。对 pretrain/post-train 接口而言，这类工作说明：模型能力提升后，瓶颈不只是会不会做任务，而是能否在长轨迹中维持自校验、自恢复和风险约束。","technical_approach":"方法上分两层。第一层是构建细粒度失败图谱。作者收集了 2,620 条真实 agent 轨迹，覆盖多家闭源与开源模型，以及多种 agent harness；人工标注每一步是正确、错误还是存在安全问题，并将 6,967 个错误归入 78 类 failure types。这里的关键不是简单打标签，而是把错误放回时间轴：记录首次错误出现的位置、后续是否恢复、错误是否被 agent 自己显式察觉、以及是否发生不可逆操作。这样才能分析“失败签名”而不是只统计错误频次。第二层是 failure localization：把‘在长轨迹中自动找出出错位置’作为一个独立能力来测。论文动机很清楚——人工逐步审查几百步轨迹不可扩展，而一个能稳定定位错误的模型，可以同时服务于调试、过程奖励、轨迹筛选和安全审计。就引言和给出的正文片段看，作者不仅做描述性统计，还试图把 failure localization 变成可评测任务，考察模型在跨域、跨环境、跨 agent harness 下的泛化。与已有工作相比，区别主要有三点：一是任务更长、更接近真实部署；二是关注 step-level failure 而不是短 benchmark 上的模块化错误分类；三是把安全违规与任务错误放在同一轨迹框架里分析，而不是只看任务完成度。","experiments":"论文的实验规模在 agent failure analysis 里算比较扎实。数据侧，作者声明收集了 2,620 条轨迹，正文前段也提到分析主体为 2,518 条轨迹、6,967 个错误、78 类失败类型，说明可能有一部分轨迹用于质控或后续 failure localization 评测。覆盖域包括 agentic software engineering、computer use、AI-for-science；模型包括 Claude、GPT-5、Gemini、DeepSeek、Qwen、Kimi；harness 包括 mini-SWE-agent、SWE-agent、OpenHands、Terminus 2 和 ReAct 风格 agent。核心经验结论有三类：第一，失败具有明显的时间结构——第一次错误之后，agent 往往不能恢复；第二，自我发现错误的比例很低，导致轨迹会在“看起来还合理”的情况下持续偏航；第三，恢复能力强依赖任务类型和环境反馈，说明不是单纯模型智力问题，而是 credit assignment 与 observability 共同决定。引言中的案例也说明 outcome metric 会漏掉‘成功但造成不可逆损害’的轨迹。由于给出的全文片段未包含后续表格与具体定位模型数值，无法可靠复述 failure localization 的精确指标；但从论文组织看，重点结果应包括：不同域的首次错误分布、恢复率/自检率统计、失败类型长尾分布，以及自动定位错误的准确率或排序质量。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着“如何给 agent 训练提供更好监督信号”去读，而不是当成普通 benchmark 论文。建议先看 Introduction，明确作者为何反对只用 final outcome 评估。然后重点看 failure taxonomy 和 annotation protocol：错误类别怎么定义，标注粒度是什么，如何区分 task error、recoverable error、safety violation、irreversible harm，这决定了数据能否用于后续 reward 或 verifier 训练。接着看描述性统计部分，尤其是首次错误位置、恢复率、自检率、不同 domain 的差异，这些结论会直接影响 process supervision 的设计。若你关心 post-train，可重点看 failure localization 任务定义、输入形式、评测指标，以及模型是否能跨 domain 泛化。预计泛读 25–35 分钟；若要把它转成训练信号设计参考，建议精看标注规范、案例分析和所有按时间展开的图表。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a gap that outcome-only agent evaluation cannot capture: where a long-horizon agent first goes wrong, whether it notices and recovers, and whether it causes irreversible harm along the way. Existing agent benchmarks mostly collapse an entire trajectory into a single pass/fail bit. That loses the temporal structure of failure. A successful final outcome may hide destructive intermediate actions, while a failed run does not tell us whether the agent made an early fatal mistake, partially recovered, or drifted silently for hundreds of steps. The paper therefore reframes evaluation from outcome-level success to trajectory-level failure mapping and failure localization across realistic long-horizon settings.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is indirect but real. It provides the kind of dense, process-level supervision signal that current long-horizon agent training lacks. That matters for reward modeling, process supervision, trajectory filtering, test-time selection, and safety auditing. If the paper’s central empirical claim holds—that agents often fail to recover after the first mistake and rarely detect their own errors—then final success rate is a systematically noisy proxy for reliability. It also means post-training signals built from outcome-only rewards are likely misaligned with what we actually want in long trajectories: self-checking, recovery, and damage avoidance. For the pretrain/post-train interface, the paper is a reminder that once raw capability improves, the bottleneck shifts toward maintaining correctness and calibration over long action sequences.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"$29","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这不是提分 paper，而是把 long-horizon agent 的失败面切开了；做 agent RL 或 verifier 的人应先看 failure taxonomy 和“首错后不可恢复”这条结论。","en":"This is not a score-chasing paper; it opens up the failure surface of long-horizon agents. LLMs are increasingly used in interactive settings by the general public, making the evaluation of model behavior in multi-turn conversational scenarios important for safety, including stereotyping-related harms. However, existing multi-turn social bias evaluations often rely on pre-specified or template-based user inputs that do not adapt to model responses and typically assume a fixed dialogue length in advance. Since raw action signals are continuous, effective tokenization is essential to map high-dimensional inputs into compact discrete tokens for autoregressive processing.
